# Working notes for The Hallowmarch

Read this before changing anything. It covers what the game is, the owner's standing requirements, how the code fits together, how to test it, and what is planned. A summary of this handoff (the status, the owner's requirements, how to build, the roadmap's latest rows and the history, with the commit whose CLAUDE.md holds the rest: `tools/handoff/make_handoff.py ... summary`) is also kept in the owner's Interego pod as the graph `urn:graph:project:hallowmarch`, updated at each milestone.

- **Owner:** Mark (GitHub `markjspivey-xwisee`)
- **Repository:** https://github.com/markjspivey-xwisee/hallowmarch (private). Work on `main`, commit often, and push at each milestone.
- **Live build:** Claude artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/QgnzHjUXkBKjCtHwb6H62T, shared with anyone who has the link. It is published from the built `hallowmarch.html` with capabilities `{db:{}, user:{}}`. When you republish to that URL, omit `capabilities` so the stored set is kept.
- **Published:** Version 15: a war that keeps moving (every band either gets on with its order or fights, soldiers come down off the walls and out of the ruins, townsfolk run to their houses' fires and beat them out, broken gates kept with the world, no band left standing behind a hall or inside a bank), the callings measured both ways round on open grass (the archers' realm ways on their own ground, Charge every 12 s, plain arrows that grow with rank), the world looked at again (houses that fall to rubble and rise behind real scaffolds, scaffolds that rise lift by lift, charred timber that cracks, holy ground that glows, firefighters with beaters, the map's names laid out), and players who know what to do on a phone first (the Chronicle folds, field notes on their own page, a marker for steps done anywhere, the war panel's aim line, no lost taps). It includes version 14: fire and siege made whole (walks, plank gates and the Thule hallow's ring that burn, garrisons that fight fire, the Logres gates passable), every skilled bot within the band, cracks and working scaffolds with masons, and bodies that move like living things in every action.
- **Next:** see the Roadmap (row 58).

## The owner's standing requirements

These apply to every change, not only to the feature that prompted them.

1. **Original work only.** The game is inspired by the three-realm war structure of the old realm-versus-realm MMOs. It uses no names, classes, places, lore, art, UI or branding from them or from any other game. Do not draw known characters or logos.
2. **Everything real and functional.** If something is drawn, it works:
   - Doors and gates open, and stairs and ramps lead up to the wall walks and towers.
   - A guard standing on a wall walked up there.
   - Nothing floats, clips through other objects or vanishes. Banners must not swing into walls.
   - Animations match what the body is doing.
3. **A reactive world:**
   - Trees can be chopped or knocked down, grass cut or set alight, and the ground cratered.
   - Walls, gates and houses break and can be repaired.
   - Houses have doors and shutters that open and close, and real interiors you can see through the windows and walk into. They can be set on fire: the fire spreads, the walls char, the roof falls in, and the house is rebuilt later.
4. **Gear lives.** Armour and clothing get dirty, wet, bloodied and damaged, and can be cleaned and repaired.
5. **Weather acts on everything.** Rain wets surfaces and cloth, snow settles on upward-facing surfaces, thunderstorms strike and set things alight, and sun dries and melts.
6. **It must look great.** Every feature ships with its own model and shader work, and visual quality is part of "done".
7. **Late game stays interesting.** The level cap is 50. Content past 20 must scale with the player, and later levels bring new abilities, ranks and goals.
8. **Players know what to do.** There is lore, onboarding and clear objectives:
   - A playtester said the armies' choice of targets "seems random" and that nobody ever wins.
   - Army orders must be visible and steerable.
   - Campaigns must be winnable.
9. **Phones too.** Every feature works with touch, and at all three quality levels (high, medium, low).
10. **Nothing clips.** No grass, flower, prop, log or body pokes through a floor, wall, roof, stair or paving, anywhere in the world. People stand on what they stand on (floors, steps, platforms), never sunk into it. Check new content for this in general, not case by case (the 1 m cover map, `natClearSpot`, `natFallHits`).
11. **Bodies move like living things.** Feet stay planted while on the ground (the gait is foot-locked IK, `HUMAN_GAIT`; beasts plant by stride and leg length), limbs never move in slow motion against the ground speed, and creatures keep moving when they idle or fly (Cinderwing beats, glides with trims, banks, looks about).
12. **Real water.** Water is physical: you can wade and swim (people and animals), ponds and rivers work (rivers flow and carry you), it splashes and ripples, puts out fire, soaks gear, and can be drunk.
13. **Deep systems, easy to play, hard to master, balanced, wired into the realms.** All of these are built (version 8); keep them deep, balanced and wired into each other:
    - class systems with real depth: an elemental magic system that reacts to the world, and systems as deep for warriors, archers and priests, each with realm flavours;
    - animals: taming, pets, livestock, breeding with inherited traits;
    - survival: food and water, hunger and thirst (gentle);
    - farming and herb lore (botany), with plant breeding;
    - crafting (materials, workstations, recipes) and building (placed structures that burn, weather and are repaired);
    - fire for everyone: a burning brand from any fire sets roofs, trees and grass alight.

## Build and run

- Run `python3 build.py`. It concatenates `src/page.html` and every `src/NN[a-z]_*.js` module in filename order into one `<script type="module">`, and writes `hallowmarch.html`. three.js 0.160.0 is imported from jsDelivr.
- All modules share one top-level scope after concatenation:
  - Top-level names must be unique across files. `tools/lint.sh` reports redeclarations.
  - No module may contain a closing script tag; `build.py` refuses to build if one does.
- Load order is filename order, but most cross-module references happen at call time. A module can use functions from later modules inside functions, not at top level.

## Testing

The harness lives in `tools/`:

- `hm.py` is a scriptable runner.
- `hmlib.py` provides `setup()`, used by the test files.
- Both load the page wrapped like the claude.ai host does, serve three.js from `tools/node_modules`, and stub web fonts.
- Chromium runs with SwiftShader (software WebGL), so expect about 30–90 s to boot and a slow frame rate.

**Workflow for any change:**

1. `tools/lint.sh` must report 0 errors. A few unused-variable warnings are known.
2. `python3 tools/tests/shaders.py`: every shader program compiles at high, medium and low, by day and by night. If you touch indoor rendering, also check inside the Barrow (see `tests/barrow.py`).
3. Write a focused test for the feature through `window.__hm`:
   - Use `__hm.simulate(seconds)` to advance game time deterministically.
   - Do not wait on wall-clock timers; they run slowly under SwiftShader.
4. Take screenshots from a fixed camera and look at them:
   - Set `__hm.UI.modal = 'menu'; __hm.CAM.mode = 'fixed'`, then position `__hm.camera`.
   - Use `page.screenshot(timeout=300000)`.
5. For war or AI changes, run `python3 tools/soak.py 10`. It must show no errors and no non-finite positions.
   For abilities, scaling and camps, run `python3 tools/tests/level50.py shots`.
6. Check the phone layout with `python3 tools/tests/phone.py`.
7. For lore and onboarding (prologue, Chronicle, path, notes), run `python3 tools/tests/lore.py` and ([guide], version 13) `python3 tools/tests/guide.py` (a new player's first steps by touch alone, and on a desk).
8. Each big system has its own test. Run the ones your change touches:
   - `castles.py`, `houses.py`, `nature.py` and `siege.py` (engines, breaches, sallies, the player's crew). [war2] `houses.py` also has a house's people beat its fire out (and the player with Use); where a house must burn down (its fall, its ruin, its rebuilding) the tests set `__hm.war2.HCREW.off` (so does `worldstate.py`)
   - `towers.py [noshots] [quality]` ([land]): the stone keeps' corner towers: eight hollow towers with three doors, two ladders and two hatches each, the old solid drum colliders stood down; the three floors carry a body at their heights with no crack between their strips; the player walks in at the courtyard door (it swings open, its collider stands down), climbs by walking into a ladder (body action 14, at the climbing pace), pushes the hatch open with E and climbs onto the top, shuts it and stands on it, opens it and goes down by walking into the hole, walks out onto a wall walk through the guard room's door; the camera stays inside the drum; a body in the store room is not named through the masonry; a new garrison archer walks up through the tower, climbing the ladder, to its post on the top. Screenshots `tower_*` (outside, store, guard room, top, the climb, night, low). [keeps] (version 11) `[only=v10,floors,tour,posts,fight,fire,shots]`: all 32 towers (8 + 4 Logres drums, 4 + 4 Ériu gate and lookout drums, 8 + 4 Thule shafts): every floor of every room carries a body with no crack, no grass in them, the old solid towers stood down, no curtain collider through a room, the furniture clear of the doors, the ladders' feet and tops and the hatches, an open hatch leaving a hole; a tour of each kind (a Logres gate drum, an Ériu gate drum and lookout, a Thule front and back corner shaft, a Thule gate shaft) as one of the keep's own: in at the courtyard door, up every ladder (pushing each hatch open with E), out through every door at the walks' height onto the walk; the camera inside a shaft, an Ériu drum and a Logres gate drum; names not through their walls; every archer of every garrison (18) walks from the courtyard to its post (the gate towers' tops first), climbing every ladder on the way; fighting on a ladder: no bow, no spell with a cast time, no charge, no shield on the rungs but a cut and an instant spell, a blow from the rungs 0.6 of one standing, a climber held by a foe at the hatch strikes it (body action 18), a heavy blow, a stun and a parry's stun knock it off, the fall's harm by its height and the wind, a foe on a shut hatch holds it and a heave that bursts it throws the foe aside, E slams the hatch on a foe coming up, a garrison archer steps onto a shut hatch and goes back after, an enemy storms a gate tower and climbs both ladders after the archer on its top; a timber tower burns (not from its own garrison's fire), chars, burns whoever is inside, falls in (gallery, breastwork and ladder down, the heap shown, whoever was up there falls) and is built again, and rain puts out a young fire (`only=fire`). Screenshots `tower_<lg|eg|el|tc|tg>_<outside|store|room|top|night|low>.png`, `tower_tc_fire.png`, `tower_tc_fallen.png` and `tower_ladder_fight.png` (`small` draws them at 800x450 on a loaded machine; `shots=tc_outside,lg_night,...` takes only those: about 4 minutes a picture at high under SwiftShader on a busy machine, so take them in two or three runs).
   - `weather.py`, `war.py` and `level50.py`
   - `war2.py [only=down,tower,island,court,engine,watch,storm,gap,stormshot,houses,beat,reset,gate] [noshots] [q=low|medium|high]` ([war2], version 15): each stall the campaign soak found, set up and watched until it moves on: a band's leader on its own keep's wall walk with an order elsewhere comes down by the castle's ways and goes on (on the ground in 2 s, 200+ m on in 90 s); one in a burh tower's ground room comes out at its door and goes on; one on a stretch of walk cut off by burnt gaps jumps into the court (hurt by the fall); a band in the court of the keep it attacks storms the court, not the gate behind it; a mangonel nobody can push is settled where it stands (or given up) within 40 s; a band whose engine works keeps its order past the 150 s watchdog; a band of four does not storm a fallen tower against the whole garrison, and goes in once a second band stands beside it; a raid on the Thule hallow goes round to a gap burnt in its ring (its gate whole) and takes the hallow; a band through a fallen tower's door into the court (screenshots `war2_storm_day_<q>`, `war2_storm_night_<q>`); townsfolk run to a burning house and beat it out (screenshots `war2_houses_day_<q>`, `war2_houses_night_<q>`); the player beats a burning house's flames down with Use beside its walls until it is out; a new campaign begins with every keep's garrison whole, one that stayed its own too; a gate burnt down is saved and loaded broken and charred. [v15] Its island, watch and storm scenes run alone (`__w2t.calm()`: the commanders frozen and every band cleared; inside the live war a passing band stormed with the small one, the engine's band breached its wall and took the keep, or the island's leader fought a foe instead of jumping, one run in two), and the watch's wall stands while its engine works.
   - `tools/campaign_soak.py [minutes=60] [low|medium|high] [chunk-seconds=300] [json=PATH] [quiet]` ([war2] measures added): an AI-only war, the player out of it in their town; per chunk the standing, the claims, the engines and (unless `quiet`) the news; every 2 s every band is looked at: it fought (a blow landed), got nearer its next point, waited on purpose (holding a keep, defending, on watch, its engine at work) or stood still, and a band still for 30 s or more is printed (`STILL`: its order and phase, where its leader stands and how high, what is round it: the nearest castle, whose it is and its gate, its engine, its stormers and crew, its leader's target and the ai states it carries), as is every band that gave its order back after 150 s (`STALL`); at the end the campaigns (how and when each was won), the share of band time in each class by order and phase, the keeps each realm held and how long it held four. Runs of one build differ a lot: read several.
   - `merged.py`: a quick smoke test of the whole game after merging branches. It checks that every system comes up, runs a stormy war for a few minutes, and takes two screenshots.
   - `integration.py`: how the version 8 systems fit together: nothing one system places (workstations, halls, paddocks, beds, plants, herbs, animals) stands in another's way or in the water; ice forms at a pond's own level and carries a body; drinking at a river fills the empty waterskins; recipes give their skins back; nothing is built on a tilled bed; three minutes with everything going.
   - `classes.py` (the callings: `mech` checks, `balance` table, `shots`) and `classes_phone.py` (the class panel and buttons on a phone). `classes.py balance` plays every calling with the simple and the skilled bot (`simple` or `skilled` alone; `levels=10,30,50`; `reps=N` for the dummy and the pack, `dreps=N` for the duels, `mreps=N` for the mastery duels; `HM_BAL=path` writes the JSON): the time to kill a standard foe (a level-matched garrison warrior), the time survived against an endless pack of three wolves and two outlaws of the level, their health and harm raised with the fighters' ability rank and gear (`rankMult`, and 8% at 20-39, 16% from 40), every newcomer 1% stronger for each second the fight has lasted ([balance2]: an endless pack of one strength left a fighter either about 30 s or the whole cap, and the median swung between them), the arena their home so they never give up the chase (capped at 150 s, a survivor scores 1.5 times), duels of every pair of callings (both on the same bot, realms swapped), and mastery duels (each calling's skilled bot against its simple bot). Fighters have a player's own measure (`ROLE_MULT.player`) and fair gear (item level = the level, every piece at the stats its slot, grade and level give on average: `fairGear`, 06e_gearcurve.js). TTK and survival are medians, so a stray arrow into a log does not swing the table. [balance3] Every level is measured under clear skies (rain damps fire, soaks foes for storms and swung a run's duels), the arenas are wiped of what the last fighters left (armed snares, runes, holy ground, steam, ice, arrows and bolts in flight), and `together` runs both bots' dummies and packs in one phase when the land has 16 arenas (half the time). `classes.py shots` adds `cls_shieldwall` (a guarded warrior walking on behind a raised shield into an archer's arrows) and `cls_dazzle` (a priest's Smite has dazzled a warrior: motes of light circle its head); `only=` picks the scenes (only those are set up and shot). `classes.py b3` (also run with `mech`): the rules of version 11 checked by number (pursuit (set aside since version 12), frost holds and deep frost, one rush, half piercing, the dazzle by rank and no parry, the shield wall's pace and NPC warriors walling against a bow, the wound and a priest's own healing by rank). [balance4] `classes.py b4` (also run with `mech`): Charge rushes the warrior to its foe and strikes it, its abilities wait for its footing and Shield Bash longer, a snare in its line catches it and holds it there, a rush reaches a foe walking away at 6 m/s and (until Leap Strike) slows it by 45% for 3 s, a skilled priest of rank I holds its mace from a warrior's guard, no rush on a horse or in the water, a guard at its post rushes only within its leash, Mend by the wound, a priest in its own Pillar of Dawn mends as its own wounds do, Rallying Cry's heal at every rank, a volley over a raised shield, the realm's way set aside; `classes.py shots only=rush` adds `cls_rush` (a warrior rushing a mage, shield forward). [balance5] `classes.py b5` (also run with `mech`): Holy Fire flares a mage's firebolt away within 10 m and not beyond, a priest steadfast from its Renewal keeps its Mend through the flare, every Renewal on yourself steadies (the fresh Renewal's rule set aside), a crippling arrow's wound takes 30% off healing while it slows, Whirlwind gives back 2% a foe a cut, a Logres archer draws 15% sooner; `classes.py shots only=hallow` adds `cls_hallow` (a warrior dazzled on a priest's holy ground: the motes over its head). `classes.py balance flat` undoes every crater before each simulated second (a mage's own meteor's bowl shields its foe from its bolts: see Balance of the callings, version 13). Every phase of `classes.py balance` and `duels.py` starts on whole ground: `CB.wipe` undoes the craters and puts out the grass fires the last fighters left (`__hm.bal5.flattenCraters`; a meteor's bowl or a leap's dent with its rim in the line of fire decided the later duels of a run). [balance6] `classes.py b6` (also run with `mech`): renewing yourself while your last Renewal still mends costs twice the power, a seasoned mage's step (12% from rank II, 2% a rank after), a Charge held on its way comes round in half its wait, a mage's ward keeps its spell from a breaking blow for a quarter of what it holds, a skilled archer takes a priest for a blade at its throat only at its mace, the Well of Ériu heals beside the Renewal; `classes.py shots only=ward` adds `cls_ward` (a mage behind its Arcane Ward in the middle of a firebolt the moment a breaking blow is taken by the ward, the warrior who struck beside it, with the HUD: the cast bar running on and "Warded" over the mage); `cls_rush`'s guard has the leash to reach the mage again (since version 12's leash rule it stood at its post and the picture showed no one). `classes.py balance`'s own duels and mastery duels fight in `CB.spotsWide` (below), and every phase has a firebreak round each arena (`CB.breakFires`; 25 m round the pack's arenas, which must stay within 170 m of the player: beasts and outlaws rest beyond it), and no arena lies within 24 m (36 m for duels) of water a fighter could kite into. [balance7] `classes.py b7` (also run with `mech`): the archers' realm ways on their own ground by number (a Logres archer on the ground and on a perch, an Ériu archer in the open and among trees, hiding there within a second where a Logres one takes 1.5 s, a Thule archer in the warm south and in Thule's winter snow: the wind on its arrows, its snare's hold and freeze, a Logres archer there as a plain one), and the rules: at 50 Charge comes round in 12 s, holds Leap Strike back the rush share (`B7T.rushShare`, 5 s) and its blow slows by 40%, plain shafts by rank (1, 1.05, 1.1, 1.15 at 20, 30, 40, 50), Mend at 95% health 0.235 of its measure and all of it at half, the tooltips naming the share and the wait. `classes.py balance`'s duels fight in the open arenas 80 m apart too (`wooded`: version 14's), and every phase's arenas are made whole: `CB.wholeGrass(x, z, r)` lets the cut, burnt and thrown-up ground within 36 m grow back at once (`__hm.nature.NAT`), from `CB.wipe` and after every duel in `duels.py`.
   - `duels.py [levels=10,30,50] [reps=24] [kind=skilled|simple|mastery|both] [pairs=warrior-priest,...] [tap] [trace=N] [cap=60] [gap=90] [narrow]` ([balance3]): the duel scope. Every pair of callings (or the pairs named) fights `reps` duels per level, realms rotated and swapped, up to 18 arenas at once ([balance6] in `CB.spotsWide`: open, dry ground anywhere on the homelands' ring at least `gap` m apart, 11 arenas at 90 m, a firebreak round each; `narrow` for version 13's 52 m round one centre; each arena runs its own queue, the next duel beginning as soon as the last ends and the arena is made whole: 576 duels at 10 in about 2 minutes, at 30 and 50 in about 8) (made exactly as `classes.py balance` makes its fighters; `HM_BAL=path` writes every duel); `kind=mastery` is each skilled bot against its simple self. With `tap`, what happened in each pairing: harm per second by ability (the auto-attack, a drawn arrow, a wound ticking, a ward biting back, or the ability last used: arrows and bolts in flight can land under the next one's name), self-healing, shields, parries, and the share of the fight stunned, rooted, slowed, casting, behind a shield, drawing, praying and in reach, and any harm from outside the duel (`FOREIGN`); `trace=N` prints a timeline of the first N duels. A whole table (three levels, 24 duels a pair) takes 3-10 minutes, much faster than `classes.py balance`'s duels. Pairs of 12 or 24 duels still move by 3 or 4 wins from run to run (and more when other work loads the machine): read a pair's number over two runs before tuning on it. [balance4] `kind=flavour` (`fbot=simple` for the simple bots): each realm's way of each calling against the same calling played plain (`e.plainCalling`: no realm's way), `reps` duels a realm a calling; with `tap` the healing is told by what gave it (`mend`, `sanctuary`, `tick` for a renewal's or holy ground's ticks, ...). Nothing is drawn: the page's own frame loop stops while it measures (`loop` keeps it), which also spares the machine (so does `classes.py balance` run alone). On a machine loaded by other work a whole skilled table took 15-25 minutes. [balance5] A page killed for memory ends the run at once (`PAGE CRASHED`; it waited for ever), and the run ends with a `rules` line: how often each rule came into play (flares, the press's healing, the skilled bots' dodges, the rushes and how they ended, dazzled misses, frost holds). [balance7] The arenas stand on open ground by default (no more than two trees within 20 m of the middle and six within 30 m), 80 m apart (`gap=80`; 9 arenas; `wooded` for version 14's 90 m and trees or not), and the meadow round an arena grows back as each duel ends (`CB.wholeGrass`: a duel's fire burnt it, and the next duels there were fought on ash, where no grass catches and no skilled bot steps out of the flames: the skilled warrior won 22 of 48 duels against a mage at 50 on ash and 9 on whole grass); the duels' scope is told for each bot kind apart (`kind=both` mixed them), and `dry` the share of a fight a side spent short of power (under 15%). **The side made first acts first in every step** (the world's lists are walked in the order bodies were made, and a duel's A side, the first calling of a pair, the realm's way in `kind=flavour`, the skilled bot in `kind=mastery`, was always made first): in a mirror of archers, both of no realm's way, the A side won 71% of 72 duels at 50, and the skilled archer beat its simple self 23 of 24 made first and 14 of 24 made second (a priest's mirror leans less, 42% and 58%). `alt` makes every other duel's B side first (half the duels each way); `kind=flavour` takes `plain=a` (the plain calling as the A side) and `plain=ab` (both plain: the measure's own lean). Read the archer's mirrors (mastery, the realm's ways) with `alt`.
   - `barrow.py` ends by sending each calling at level 28 with fair gear and three healing draughts against the Hollow King, the player played by the skilled bot.
   - `worldstate.py`: a busy land (felled, split and regrowing trees, cut and burning grass, a crater with loot in it, burning, ruined and rebuilding houses, breached, rebuilding and worn walls) is saved, the page reloaded with the same storage, and everything checked; then an old save and a damaged one. Prints the save sizes.
   - `water.py`: the world check (every river course has water, no dry pond beds, no road through deep water), wading and swimming numbers for the player, a soldier and a wolf, the current, the camera above water, no casting while swimming, a rider sliding off, fire out, drinking and the waterskin; `water.py shots [medium]` adds screenshots (shore, river, swimming, wake, wading).
   - `building.py [noshots] [hall|build]`: each realm's hall door, walking in, the war table's command grant (and an enemy keep's table until it is taken), the armourer, basin, hearth and stores; building: refused places, a foundation rising in stages, snapping and turning, a cottage with a door and a roof that keeps the rain off, a bed as the return point, fire spreading and falling in, repair, pulling down, a fence with a gate, save and load; screenshots of the halls, the war table, the ghost and the cottage.
   - `survival.py`: needs over simulated time by activity and their effects (never death), eating and drinking, a well and a lake, foraging and regrowth, a hunt's drops (more with a knife), fishing (too soon, in time, too late), cooking and a stew, a recipe at every station, a crafted pick and axe used, merchants, the Chronicle and help, the save (old and damaged too); screenshots of the HUD needs, a bush, a mushroom ring, fishing, the forge, the crafting book on a desk and a phone. `noshots` skips the pictures.
   - `farming.py`: the season cycle and its weather; tilling, sowing and growth through every stage; harvest yields; frost, drought, weeds; crossing two strains and the offspring's traits; seed saving raising the yield over generations; the herbal; trampling and fire; the townsfolk working the fields; save and load (old and damaged saves); screenshots of beds at each stage after rain, the plant card, a townsfolk harvest, a ripe field and a herb patch in shade. `noshots` skips the screenshots, `shotsonly` takes only them, `phone` adds the plant card on an upright phone with touch.
   - `seasons.py [noshots] [final]`: each season's curves and uniforms, a broadleaf crown and the meadow read off the screen through the year, bare winter crowns in the canopy map (pines keep theirs), snow lying by temperature (Thule and the heights all winter, the south white after a cold night and green by day, the spring thaw), the weather agreeing (rain in a Thule summer, snow in its winter), Thule's lake frozen and walked across (a wolf too, no splash, fishing through a hole) and swum in summer, the thaw from the shallows, the rivers and the southern lakes never frozen, the gardens of Ériu and Thule worked by their townsfolk, no tree in a field, fewer birds in winter, butterflies and fireflies in season, the field notes and the Chronicle; screenshots of the Logres orchard in all four seasons and a winter dawn, an Ériu glen in autumn, the frozen Thule lake with the player on it, the gardens.
   - `animals.py`: taming a wolf step by step, commands and a hound fighting, taming and riding a wild horse, livestock producing, two generations of breeding, save and load; `animals.py shots` adds screenshots (hound, flock, cattle, hens, mare and foal, the animal card).
   - `depth.py [noshots] [only=nature|birds|craft|build|animals] [shotsonly]`: bushes burning from a spell, the grass and a brand, growing back; a bush cut down; an apple tree chopped, falling and cut up; a stump grubbed out and the ground free to build and till; the save (and damaged ones); pheasants and ducks flushing, flying off and landing; a duck shot down; the drawn bow hitting a bird; a hen plucked; grave-iron, scales and trophies; tier 2 and 3 item levels, a named masterwork, the mark through the save, the draughts and produce recipes, the master's bonus stat; hip roofs, a lean-to, leaded windows, a well (drink, troughs); a townsman through a built door; the garrison sparing its roofs; a clutch hatching, a fertile egg, moulting, a hound herding sheep, the trough's skins. Screenshots `depth_*`.
   - `gifts.py`: each realm's passive gifts by their numbers, each realm ultimate (effects, cooldown, key 9, HUD, a champion using it), the Chronicle page, the phone pad; screenshots of each ultimate.
   - `motion.py [tag=...] [only=human,beast,farm,bird,player,pose,dragon,shots]`: every kind of body measured from what its shader draws (`__hm.motion`): people at 0.4 to 7 m/s and turning on the spot, wolves, boars, bears, deer, horses, sheep, cattle, pigs, hounds, hens, pheasants and mallards at a crawl, a walk, a trot and a gallop, the player running, sprinting, up a keep's stair, riding (the horse's hooves) and swimming (how far a stroke carries), a kneel and a crouch (feet and knees over the floor), [polish] a person walking while shoved sideways and standing shoved sideways, a wolf and a horse trotting shoved sideways, the player cutting 46 and 92 degrees at a sprint and 46 at a run (`MO.shove`, `MO.cut`), the feet on the stair's treads (`motion_tread.js`: where a foot stands still, its height over the floor under it: on its tread about 0, on the flight's slope line up to half a riser off), every lane clear of anything standing on each body's own line (the cover map: a rack on a lane slid the runner along it mid-measure), and Cinderwing on its circuit, taking off and landing (`motionDragon`, its bones read directly: wingbeats a second, how far a wing tip travels in a beat, the share of time gliding, its bank, how far its head and tail move, its airspeed and the hardest change of it, its feet on the ground once landed). For each body: how far a planted foot slides (cm), its slip as a share of the body's speed (slipK), how high a swinging foot rises, the lowest foot over the floor (5th percentile and lowest), steps a second; the worst offenders listed; writes `tools/out/motion_<tag>.json`; `shots` adds pictures at 960x540 (a pheasant bursting up, beating and gliding, a mallard running across the water and beating, Cinderwing rearing, springing, flying and landing, a runner, a kneeling priest: `tools/out/motion_*.png`); [polish] `seq` adds strips of six frames 1/20 s apart (a horse at a gallop and a bear at a trot from the side with the camera keeping pace, the player cutting 90 degrees at a sprint from a fixed camera: `tools/out/motion_seq_*.png`, at `q=high` unless `q` says otherwise); [motion3] usable at high quality: the page's own frame loop is stopped first (it drew whole frames at high between the strip's own) and one frame drawn to compile the high programs, the lane is mown (`natureCut`) so the legs show over the grass, the camera on whichever side of the lane has no tree or plant near it (`MS.pickSide`), and up a stair the camera behind the player while it steps (it walks the way the camera looks) and abreast and above it for the picture; strips of nine frames of the gallop (a whole stride), the horse's trot and the player walking up a keep's stair from the side, six of the bear and the cut; `seq=gallop,trot,bear,cut,stair` picks them (about a minute a frame at high on a busy machine). Run it before and after anything that moves a body.
   - `gaits.py [only=falls,feet,stair,shove,cuts,crowd] [reps=3] [cuts=26]` ([motion3]; [motion4] `cuts`: the player cutting 46, 92 and 135 degrees at a sprint and 46 and 92 at a run, the cut started at every frame of a whole stride, 26 runs a case, every run's worst stance under 2 cm): the footfalls of a horse's walk (the lateral sequence in four even beats), trot (diagonal pairs together, within a frame of each other: a real trot's pairs part by up to 30 ms) and gallop (hind right, hind left, fore right, fore left, the fore right down before the hind left lifts, one leap a stride after the fore feet); no foot near the ground moving over it (lift-off and touchdown too, within 4 cm of where each foot stands: every beast at a walk and a trot, people running, 1.5 cm at most); a person up a keep's stair at a walk and at a run with no foot in a tread or a riser, every frame; shoves and cuts run after run, every run's worst stance under its limit; 400 bodies near the camera all with their feet held (more than 256) and the lock's JS time a step.
   - `actions.py [only=ladder,blow,shove,rush,stop,row,kneel] [noshots] [shots=climb,climb_back,shove,row,rush] [tag=...]` ([motion4], version 14): every action since version 10 measured as a body from what the shaders draw (13r_motion.js's probe, its feet and now its hands: a fist's own points, none another vertex shares): a person climbing a Logres corner tower's ladder (every foot standing on a rung while it holds, its sole on the rung's top and the ball of the foot over it; every hand round a rung while it holds; each limb holds still on its rung 10 to 14 frames at a time), striking from the rungs (the feet on theirs through every blow), the warrior's Charge (its stride's worst stance at 18 m/s, and its landing: no foot moving over the ground), the player halting from a run and from a sprint, six stops across the stride each (a foot near the ground moves at most 2 cm), shoving a beached boat off (the feet step into the stance and stand), at the oars (each hand from its oar's grip, every frame of three strokes, the grips worked out as boatsDraw swings and dips the oars out in the rowlocks: 90% of the frames within 8 cm), a kneeling body (its front foot on the floor, its back knee on it). Pictures (unless `noshots`, 960x540): `act_climb` and `act_climb_back` (a climber half way up a corner tower's ladder, from beside it in the room), `act_shove`, `act_row`, `act_rush`. Writes `tools/out/actions_<tag>.json`.
   - `motion5.py [only=birds,gallop,heel,hammer,jump,rubble,shove,dive] [tag=run] [q=low] [shots]` ([motion5], version 16): what versions 14 and 15 added and what was still off, as bodies from what the shaders draw. `birds`: a hen, a cock pheasant and a mallard walking and running (each planted foot's worst stance in contact within 1.5 cm, a bird's leg being a fifth of a person's; no foot moving over the ground within 1.2 cm of it; the feet on the ground; how high a foot lifts; the head-bob: the share of each step the beak holds still in the air, its speed along the way under a quarter of the body's). `gallop`: a horse at 4.5, 8, 11 and 13 m/s: the share of the time no foot is down, the longest leap as a share of the stride, each foot's share down, the planted feet. `heel`: a person at 0.6 to 5 m/s: how high the heel rises before the toes leave the ground, and how far the point each foot stands on (its lowest sole point, frame to frame: `soles`, 13r_motion.js) moves over the ground in a stance (at most 1 cm), the sole's middle as motion.py measures it. `hammer`: a builder at three houses of Brackenford brought down and being built back, hammering (action 23) at its work spot: the mallet's farthest point at the blow against the wall's face (a shot along its facing), the feet. `jump`: a soldier jumping down from a keep's wall walk into its court as war2's leaders do (a walk whose inner edge drops into the court, the court cleared of bodies round it): no foot in the ground through the landing (a trace of the frames round it), none sliding once down. `rubble`: a Logres corner tower brought down, the player walked (and run) from the field over the rubble into the store room: each planted foot (still in all three axes) against the stone drawn under its sole (`__hm.motion5.rubbleFootFloor`, the highest under heel, middle and toes), with the feet on the stones and with them on the ramps as before (`RUBHF.off`). `shove`: where every beached boat's gunwale lies for a shover at her end (`shoveGeo`: ahead, up over its floor, across), then shoving one off (her drawn pose kept up with her place each step, `__hm.motion5.boatPose`): each hand's lowest point over her gunwale's cap where the hand is along her (from her pose that step), from 0.15 to 0.5 of the action (her first 15 cm away; then the arms straighten after her and come away), by tenths of the action with the hands and the gunwale in the shover's frame, and the feet. `dive`: a diver swimming on under a lake: how far a stroke carries it, strokes a second, the hands' reach ahead and behind. Writes `tools/out/motion5_<tag>.json`; `shots` takes `m5_*.png` at 960x540.
   - `perf.py [low|medium|high] [views=...] [tag=...] [js|render] [profile]`: draw calls, triangles (main and shadow pass) and programs in fixed views (the Logres town with its fields, a keep courtyard, inside its hall, a river, the biggest fight of a running war, a forest, the Barrow, the mountains from the Logres orchard) at each quality, the top meshes by triangles (instances x triangles each, named by the function that made them), the JS cost of a step and a frame and the top systems by JS time (every update and draw function timed), the boot's steps and the heap; `profile` adds a sampling profile of the simulation. Writes `tools/out/perf_<tag>.json`. Run it before and after anything that draws or thinks more.
   - `hud_layout.py [touch|desk] [noshots] [sizes=WxH,...] [notch]`: every panel up at once (needs and gift marks, each calling's class panel, the breath bar, the companion bar with two companions, the builder's bar, the plant card, a target, the path tracker, a toast and a field note) at 390x780, 360x640, 844x390 and 667x375 with touch and 1280x720, 960x540 and 1920x1080 on a desk: no two panels overlap, nothing is off the screen or under the notch (`notch` emulates safe areas), every touch control takes a 40 px cross of taps; one screenshot per size.
   - `mountains.py [tag] [views=...] [q=high|medium|low] [noshots] [nochecks] [small]` ([land]): the land as version 10 left it, before the whole mountain is shaped (`__hm.heights.HTS.H0`/`walk0`: walk mask, heights within two texels of any walkable texel, roads, camps, colliders, trees and rocks), against version 9 (`mountains_base.json`), and after the shaping ([heights]): what anyone can reach, the roads, camps, colliders, the trees there were and the rocks unchanged, no height changed outside the shaped ground, no reachable texel blocked and no blocked texel opened, the crags (on the mountains, none over or next to walkable ground, none in the water, none floating), no hollow on the reshaped ground, the camera never inside a crag; screenshots `mtn_<tag>_<view>.png` from the Logres orchard (day, dusk, winter, low), each realm's pass, a frontier road, a keep's wall walk (high and low), the foot of a big face (high and low), Thule in winter, Ériu from its town, a crag up close (high and low), and ([heights]) the range across the frontier from a Logres keep (day, dusk, low), the Logres and Thule towns looking at their mountains (Thule in winter too) and the hardy trees (high and low). `tag=base` with `HM_HTML` set to another build writes the base.
   - `heights.py` ([heights]): the shaping's numbers and time at boot; the land before it is version 10's; every road point, site, camp and water keeps its heights; every texel anyone could reach is as walkable as it was and nothing new opens (the unreachable texels now too steep are printed); the ring's skyline has more peaks and more rise and fall along its crest than before; every tree, rock, plant and herb stands on the ground; the hardy trees: enough of them, after every tree there was, on walkable mountain ground, none above the snow line, in water, on a road or in a collider, each with its nature state, collider and chunk instance; the outcrops: enough, each with its collider, none by a road, in water, floating or on a tree, plant or herb; one tree is chopped down, split and grows a sapling, one burns out; the canopy map shades the ground under them; snow lies on the new high ground in a Thule winter.
   - `heights_save.py` (`HM_V10=` a version 10 build): version 10 makes a busy land (trees felled by number, one on a mountain's foot, a burning tree, a crater on a mountain slope, a grass fire) and saves; this build loads it: no errors, the same trees felled lying the same way, the crater back, the character continues and the world runs on.
   - `polish.py [noshots] [shotsonly] [only=boats,tracks,drift,lock] [small]` ([polish]): a swimmer pushes a free boat away (she turns when pushed off her middle, the swimmer never inside her hull, her hull never over land), a moored boat does not give, Use at a beached boat's shore end shoves her off (action 19) and she floats free, a boat drifting in to the shore lies still (largest step under 1 mm); prints laid in the snow are saved, cleared and laid again where they were and as filled as they were (an old save and a damaged one too, and the part's size); the drifts are in the grass's map; a shoved body's planted feet are held. Screenshots `polish_prints_high.png`, `polish_prints_low.png`, `polish_boat_push.png`, `polish_boat_shove.png`, `polish_drift.png`.
   - `look.py [views=a,b,...|group] [q=high,medium,low] [v=day,dusk,night,dawn,rain,storm,mist,snow,spring,summer,autumn,winter] [tag=now] [w=640] [h=360] [noshots] [nochecks] [list]` ([look], version 12): the whole world as a player sees it. The views are in `tools/tests/look_views.js` (`window.LK.views`, by group: towns, close, keeps, sites, frontier, nature, water, mountains, bodies, barrow, dragon; `list` prints them): each town's street (the play camera behind the player in the plaza), a house's front, a house inside, the town from a rise, the fields, the jetty; a house wall at 3.5 m, a round house's thatch from under its eave, an oak's crown over a low camera, two people at 2 m, a priest and a warrior walking; each keep's gate, courtyard, wall walk, a corner tower's store, room and top, the hall, the keep from afar; the temples, musters and the hill; a champion's camp, a bandit camp, the bandit camp whose tents stood round a tree, a road, a pass, the biggest fight; a forest, a forest's edge, a meadow; a river, a lake, a pond, under a lake, swimming; the mountains from the orchard, a crag, the range; each realm's line-up of the four callings, the camps' beasts, the farm beasts, a rider, a fight, the townsfolk; the Barrow; Cinderwing. Each view is a camera fixed by the land (or the player stood there and the play camera behind), the HUD hidden; `v=` lays a time of day, weather or season on top. Writes `tools/out/look_<tag>_<view>_<q>_<variant>.png`. The checks (`LK.check()`, unless `nochecks`), in general over the whole world: no tree's trunk in anything built (walls, houses, fences, props, tents, fire pits: a tree by a boulder or a standing stone is counted, not failed), on a road or in deep water, no crown in a house; no rock in anything built or on a road; no plant, herb or workstation in anything built; no town prop in a house; no grass through a floor near the ground; no grass in the water (every 1 m point under 5 cm of water or more); trees, rocks, plants and herbs neither floating nor sunk (over 0.25-0.35 m); no person sunk into or floating over the floor under them (`floorAt`), no body inside anything solid. `look.py serve` boots once and runs the commands dropped as JSON in `tools/out/look_cmd/` (`{"js": "...", "shot": "name", "q": "low"}`; `{"reload": true}` reads the views again; `{"quit": true}`): a look at many views costs one boot.
   - `walls.py [noshots] [only=geo,arrows,proj,slit,fire,crenel,stone,perf,breach,save,shots] [quality] [shots=...]` ([walls], version 12): the swept test (`shotHit`) against a Logres curtain (stone; a shot well over it goes on), a Thule palisade (logs), a town house's wall (plaster) and its window (stopped by its shutters shut, in through it open), a roundhouse's thatch, a log house's wall and shingles, a built thatch roof (from above, and a shot under it goes on), a tower's drum (stone) beside an arrow slit and straight into it, its door shut and open, a crenellated parapet (a merlon and the breastwork stop a shot, a crenel above the breastwork lets it by); the player's drawn arrows stick in a palisade and glance off a curtain (and come to lie within a few metres of its foot), a foe behind the curtain not struck; an NPC archer holds a shot that would only strike a house (its `ai.wallShot` set) and lands it once the line is clear; an archer at a store room's slit shoots out through it, a pace beside the slit cannot; an enemy's fire bolt bursts in a roundhouse's thatch and sets it alight, the realm's own spares it; a garrison archer on a Logres gate tower's top shoots out through its crenel at a foe below and the foe shoots back where it shows, a body standing back on the top covered; a wall archer at its post on a corner tower's top with a foe off to the tower's side close below steps up to the crenel facing it (about 2.3 m), shoots, and walks back to its post when the foe is gone; a siege stone meets the tower in its way; the cost of a test (about 3 us in the open, 15-60 us at a keep's wall under SwiftShader on a loaded machine). [breach] all 20 stone towers are sections (Logres 6 a keep, Ériu 4); stones crack one (wear) and bring its upper works down (8 of a trebuchet's): its rooms, ladders, hatches and the doors at the walks stand down, the archer on its top and a guard in its room fall into the rubble hurt by the fall, a mound of rubble spills out over the fallen face; the player climbs in over the rubble, through the store room and out at its courtyard door; the commanders count its danger (0.9) and ([siege2]) send their bands in over its rubble; in peace it is mended behind scaffolding (about 150 s) with its rooms back; a breached gate tower and a cracked lookout saved, the page reloaded, both as they were. `shots`: `walls_stuck` (arrows in a house's plaster), `walls_glance` (arrows glanced off a curtain's outer face lying at its foot), `walls_breach_day`, `_night`, `_low`, `walls_breach_mend` (behind its scaffold), `walls_charred` (a burnt Thule tower smouldering), `walls_charred_dusk`, `walls_charred_cold`, `walls_gallery_high` and `_medium` (a gallery's floor in the sun: no lines of light between its boards), `walls_hatch` (a lid without seams across its boards), `walls_ladder_fight` and `walls_ladder_fight_below`. The page's own frames are held (`__rafHold`), so evaluates do not wait behind them, and `shot()` lets go of the particles born while simulating (see Gotchas).
   - `look2.py [noshots] [only=paths,climb,hound] [shots=view:q:variant,...] [tag=now]` ([look2], version 13): the towns' footpaths in the 1 m path map (every path's middle full, 3 m off it bare: `__hm.look2.path`); a body on a ladder (action 14) carries its blade and shield on its back, read back from what the shader draws (13r_motion.js's probe: the blade's tip and the shield's middle in the body's frame), and in its hands again off the ladder, and in a blow from the rungs (18) the blade is in the hand and the shield on the back; a hound by a hearth at night is lit warm by the room (the picture's pixels at the hound: red over blue); no runtime errors. Then the `look2` group's views of `look_views.js` (`shots=view:quality:variant,...`; by default the hound at night, the hand-over at high and medium, the crown at low, the orchard in summer and winter, the town's paths from a rise, a street in snow, the climb). The `look2` group also holds `climb_back` (the climber from the side: he climbs between the ladder and the wall facing the room, so his back is seen in profile) and version 12's additions as a player meets them: `stuck_house`, `stuck_logs`, `glance`, `breach`, `breach_in`, `mend`, `charred`, `rush` and `gaits` (a horse galloping, a wolf trotting and a soldier running past, from abreast); look at them with `look.py views=look2` or `look.py serve`.
   - `look3.py [noshots] [only=scaffolds,masons,gates,halls,cracks,hound] [shots=view:q:variant,...] [tag=now]` ([look3], version 14): every scaffold of a curtain, a stone tower and a burnt palisade gap has a whole run of standards, bays, a floor of boards at each lift and a ladder up to each lift (`look3Scaffold`, 04d_look3.js; a curtain's inner run may be empty where a walk's deck stands over it), none of its standards in a collider, a tree or under a raised floor (`look3Clear`); a body of each keep's realm walks out through its open gate; a breached tower's scaffold goes up, its boards are floors and its ladders climbable ([look4] its first lift's: the lifts above go up with the work), two masons come out of the keep's hall, walk out by the gate and round the walls to it, one works at its foot and the other climbs its ladder and works on the first lift's boards (each on the floor under it, hammering), and when the work is about done the one above climbs down and both go home into the hall; every keep's hall has its footpath from its door to the inside of the gate (the 1 m path map full along it, the cover map bare, 3 m off it untouched); a worn curtain, a worn tower and a battered house drawn with the new cracks (the shaders compile); a hound by a hearth at night lit warm by the room; no runtime errors. Then the `look3` group's views of `look_views.js` (`shots=view:quality:variant,...`; by default the cracked tower and curtain, a breached tower's store room, the scaffolds of a tower and of a palisade gap, the masons at work, the palisade built back with pale logs, a fallen cone, a cracked house, the halls' paths, the hound at night, the crown at low, the hand-over at medium). The `look3` group: `crack_curtain`, `crack_curtain_far`, `crack_tower`, `scaffold_tower`, `masons`, `scaffold_curtain` (a Logres side wall), `scaffold_timber` (a Thule palisade's stretch), `pal_scaffold`, `pal_fresh`, `cone_fallen`, `house_cracked`, `hall_path0`..`2`.
   - `look4.py [noshots] [only=ruin,scaffold,ladder,lifts,map,beater] [shots=view:q:variant,...] [tag=now]` ([look4], version 15): a house of each realm brought down by stones lies in rubble (`houseBreak`: its walls standing 0.3-1.4 m, down from the corners to where the stones broke them most, their broken tops closed and the steps' ends filled, the masonry heaped, the shader told the walls' measures and that it fell), its walls' colliders cut down to 1.6 m, a shot at 2 m crossing its broken wall and one at 0.25 m striking it; built back behind a working scaffold (standards solid, boards floors, a ladder climbed onto the boards), its walls rising course by course (the shader told the level) and the new roof frame on them once they stand, the townsfolk at work inside the scaffold at the wall, clear of anything by the house; whole again with the scaffold down and the colliders whole; a breached tower's scaffold rising lift by lift with the work (each lift drawn as it goes up, the first ladder's rungs with it: made again, it once showed its standards alone), the mason above climbing on to each lift; a climber's feet and hands on a scaffold ladder's rungs (13r_motion.js's probe, as `actions.py`'s climb); the fire beater's part bit; the world map's names at 1280x720 and 390x780: none over another, every town, keep and realm named (and `look4_<tag>_map_<w>x<h>.png`). Then pictures of the `look4` group of `look_views.js` (by default the three ruins at high, medium and low, a house's scaffold, a burning plank gate up close, a burning log tower's roof from close above, holy ground, a soldier with the fire beater, a climber on a scaffold's ladder). The `look4` group: `ruin0`..`2` and `ruin_top0`..`2` (a town house of each realm brought down by stones, from its street and from above), `house_scaf0`..`2` (the same a third of the way through being built back), `gate_burn` (a burh's plank gate burning, at 3 m), `gate_fallen`, `tower_roof` (a burning Thule tower's shingles from 4 m above), `hallow` (a priest's holy ground under a dazzled warrior), `beaters` (the garrison beating out a palisade fire), `beater_close` (a soldier with the fire beater, close), `scaf_climb`, `deck_gap`, `deck_fire`, `deck_fresh` (a burh's walk burnt through, burning at night, built back), `ring` (the Thule hallow's ring burnt through), `roof_in` and `roof_out` (a holed roof from inside by day and from above).
   - `siege2.py [noshots] [only=mark,storm,stormall,palisade,cone,house,door,archers,save,shots] [quality] [small]` ([siege2], version 13): a band's trebuchet takes a cracked Logres corner tower for its mark (`siegeMarks`: the weaker way in) and its stones crack it further; a band of ten storms a fallen Logres corner tower with no garrison and no lord (the keep cannot fall): every member over the rubble, through the store room and out at its courtyard door into the court, one or two inside the narrow part at once, nobody standing stuck (the longest stand of any body while it has somewhere to go), then an Ériu lookout with its garrison at home: the garrison plugs the court before its door, the stormers go for no foe up on the walls and fight their way out into the court (with six guards plugging it, four to seven of the ten came through, in 27 to 66 s over four runs); `stormall`: a Logres gate drum, an Ériu gate drum, an Ériu lookout and a Logres corner drum each stormed by ten (all through in 9.5-15 s, nobody standing); a Thule palisade spared by its own garrison's fire, caught by an enemy soldier's fire spells only now and then (3 or 4 of 16) and always by the player's, charred, the fire spreading along the wall, burnt through into a gap a body walks through into the court and the bands storm (danger 0.9), rain putting out a young fire on it, built back behind scaffolding in peace and its charred logs replaced (pale for a time); an Ériu gate tower's thatched cone spared by its own side, set alight by an enemy's fire spell, falling in (the platform and its ladder stand, the charred thatch heaped on it) and thatched again; a town house cracked by a stone at its wall, holed by one from above (the rain falls in under the hole and not elsewhere), brought down by four trebuchet stones to a ruin of rubble (not burnt, no smoulder) and built back as after a fire, a keep's hall taking six; an arrow shot into a closed door swinging with it as it opens and back as it shuts; a garrison archer with a foe off its tower's side and an attacking archer under a curtain with a defender back on the walk, the old way and the new (`LINE.on`): harm done, held looks; a burnt gap, a burning stretch, a holed house, a ruin and a fallen cone saved and loaded. `shots`: `siege2_storm_high` and `_low` (a band climbing in over a fallen tower's rubble), `siege2_palisade_night` (burning), `siege2_palisade_gap_high` and `_low` (burnt through), `siege2_cone_dusk` (a burning cone), `siege2_house_holed`, `siege2_house_ruin`.
   - `siege3.py [noshots] [only=deck,gate,temple,crew,player,ring,roof,door,gatepast,stormcost,shots,save] [quality] [reps=N]` ([siege3], version 14): a burh's wall walk catches from the player's fire (its own garrison's spares it), burns with real flames, chars, burns whoever stands on it, spreads along the walk, burns through (its floor stands down, a body on it falls and is hurt, charred boards lie below, a way along the walk over the gap is no route), is put out by rain and built back in peace; a burh's gate of planks burns, chars and falls (its door dies: everyone may pass; the news says it burnt down) and is hung again; the timber hallow's gate burns only once its realm has lost a keep; the garrison sees a fire on its palisade and on its walk and sends people to it (they climb onto the walk), who beat it out, leaving their posts, and come back down to their posts' floor after; the player's Use beside a fire beats it down (body action 21) and puts it out; the timber hallow's log ring burns through (its wall, walk and breastwork stand down) and is built back; a stone holes a roof and broken rafters lie across the hole, and the houses' shadow pass cuts it; an arrow in a built door's leaf swings with it and falls with the piece; `gatepast`: a band goes in by a broken gate past a fallen gate tower (Ériu and Logres) with nobody standing stuck while it has somewhere to go; `stormcost` (`reps=N`): a band of ten storms each stone tower kind's breach against the whole garrison and its lord (prints `STORM` lines: through, attackers left, defenders before and after, the keep taken); `shots`: `siege3_gate_dusk` (low), `siege3_deck_gap` (high), `siege3_crew` (low; its framing is not right yet: the picture comes out dark, the camera in a room or a wall's shade), `siege3_roof_in` and `_out` (high), `siege3_ring` (medium), `siege3_deck_night` (low); `save`: a burning stretch of walk, a gap and one being built back saved and loaded.
   - `guide.py [phone] [desk] [noshots]` ([guide], version 13): players know what to do. On a phone (390x780, touch only: taps, swipes and the stick; `window.__hm` only sets the scene): the title screen tapped through, the prologue turned by swipes, the first-steps note first and on the screen clear of every panel (and a note at 360x640, 844x390 and 667x375), the path's next step marked on the minimap (the gold gem's pixels, or the arrow on the rim) with how far and which way in the tracker, the stick walking you toward it, the herald by Use and the bounty moving the mark, a note held in a fight and one about the fight shown in it, a tap dismissing a note, the watches of the new notes, the Chronicle's path (24 steps), Aims, Gear and Sieges pages and How to play on a phone (no overflow, taps not keys), every new step of the path taken through the world's own state and paid (a meal, a thing made, a tower's top, an order, a keep, the realm's gift, a masterwork, a campaign, Cinderwing), an old save gaining the new early steps quietly (path version 2). On a desk: the first note points to H, H and J, the help's keys, the war page's link to Sieges, the map's mark, a note clear of the panels. Screenshots `guide_phone_start`, `guide_phone_path`, `guide_phone_note`, `guide_phone_aims`, `guide_phone_help`, `guide_desk_map`, `guide_desk_note`.
   - `guide2.py [phone] [desk] [noshots]` ([guide2], version 15): players know what to do, on a phone first. Phone (390x780, touch; `window.__hm` sets the scene): every Chronicle page measured natural and folded at 390x780, 360x640 and 844x390 (printed; `tools/out/guide2_pages.json`), none wider than the sheet, every page folded under 2.4 of its sheet on an upright phone and 3.5 on one on its side; a calling's card folded under its head and first lines, a tap opening your own and it staying open when the page comes back, Open all and Fold all, a folded page's words still in the page; your path's page (the steps walked and the rest folded, the step at hand open, the link to the field notes); the field notes for what came since given through the world's own state (a keep's hall, a scaffold's ladder, a brand's prompt at a palisade, a house battered near you, a priest's Renewal on itself, a mage's ward, a warrior's rush, dazzled, wounded, chilled) and the farmers' word a note by a town field; a garrison running to a fire near you told by a toast; a note at 360x640 (shrunk to its title, a tap opens the field notes with itself first and open); a place for the late levels, a war table for an order below 20 (on the frontier), the map button for one from 20, the gift's button, a masterwork's lack, the keep a campaign is fought for; the path's next step "command" glowing on the map button with the tracker's line; the war panel's line for your aim and a tap on the panel opening it; Cinderwing's step only for a real share (a single blow from 150 m no, a fifth of its harm yes); eight taps on the companion bar's Stay and Follow by CDP touch while a toast comes and the class panel grows under the finger, none lost and the bar held still. Desk (960x540): the Callings page folds with Open all, a short page does not, the war panel's aim clicked. Screenshots `guide2_phone_callings`, `guide2_phone_path`, `guide2_phone_notes`, `guide2_phone_war`, `guide2_desk_callings`, `guide2_desk_war`.
   - `touch_flows.py [only=...]`: a phone with touch alone drinks at a river, fishes (cast and strike), swims out with the stick, dives (the pad's Dive) and comes up (Up), steps into a boat at a jetty, rows it with the stick and goes over the side (Dive in), tills and sows from the plant card, crafts from the book at a workbench, picks, turns and places a building piece, orders a companion, tames a wolf, uses a keep hall's war table ([land] and goes into a keep's corner tower and climbs it hatch by hatch: `only=tower`; [keeps] a Logres gate tower and a Thule corner tower the same way, `only=gatetower,timbertower`, and a fight at a hatch, `only=ladder`: a foe comes up the ladder and a tap on Use slams the hatch on it, and on the rungs a drag of the stick down turns you back; [polish] shoves a beached boat into the water with Use and pushes her with the stick: `only=shove`; [siege2] holds a burning brand to an enemy burh's palisade and a tap on Use sets it alight: `only=palisade`; [guide] opens your path and its Aims from the path tracker and taps a field note away: `only=guide`; [war2] beats a burning house's flames down with Use beside its walls until it is out (its people kept away): `only=housefire`); no name shows through the hall's walls. [guide2] Its `tap()` lays the HUD out and holds it still from its measure until the tap's own click lands (`G2.tap.until`, 30 s at most, let go at the tap's end): the page's own frames ran a layout pass between the measure and the touch now and then (the companion bar's Stay or Follow came to nothing three runs in five on version 14's builds; with a hold of 4 s, once more in this work's last full run, when the machine's frames took longer than that between the measure and the finger); the companion flow prints what each press and click met and where the bar stood (`TFEV`) if a tap comes to nothing. [v15] Alone it runs 40 to 60 minutes on a machine loaded by other runs (the suite's 50-minute limit cut it off once): give it an hour and more, or run it in parts with `only=`; the field note's tap is read on the note tapped (`#tf-note`: a note queued meanwhile came up in the seconds a tap's waits take), the hatch's slam by `LADDER.stats.slams`, and the scaffold waits with no band about the keep (a breach draws the commanders' bands now).
   - `loot.py`: [balance2] the item curve and the late toughness by level, the item levels of camp foes', champions', soldiers', keep lords' and Cinderwing's drops, the Barrow's burial chest and the Hollow King's hoard and the merchants at levels 20, 35 and 50, a named masterwork against a drop of its grade and level (+16-19%), an old save's gear loading with its own levels, and per blow how many blows a player in fair gear needs to fell a garrison warrior and a keep lord at 20, 35 and 50 (each calling), the Hollow King at 28 and Cinderwing at 50, and how many they need, with the old curve and the new (k = the new ratio over the old, within ±0.1).
   - `water2.py [noshots] [shotsonly] [only=dive,finds,ice,boats,fisher,tracks] [shots=under,boat,river,tracks,drift,ice] [medium] [small]` ([water2]): diving and coming up, the bed solid, a still diver rising, the underwater stroke, breath running out and the water harming, the breath bar, drowning and release, the current carrying a diver, no diving in the shallows; every kind of find taken and saved; a hole cut in thick ice, the ice holding a diver under, harm without breath, up at the hole; a boat boarded from a jetty, rowed (speed, the stroke, glide, turning), run aground and stepped out of, never pushed over land, carried by a river, solid to a swimmer, shouldering a still swimmer aside, a diver under her keel kept below it, climbed into from the water and tied up, fished from, burnt and rebuilt; a fisher rowing out in the morning and back with the catch for the stores; prints in snow filled by new snow, in mud dried away, cut by a later print, paws and hooves, wet sand, drifts only where snow lies. Screenshots `water2_under.png`, `water2_under_bed.png`, `water2_boat_lake.png`, `water2_boat_river.png`, `water2_tracks_snow.png`, `water2_under_ice.png`.
9. In test scripts, a `page.evaluate` must never return `window.__hm` or a helper object that holds it. Playwright tries to serialise the whole game and stalls. End helper scripts with `;0`.

**Debug API (`window.__hm`, defined at the end of `13_main.js`):**

- `ready` and `errors`: the boot flag and every runtime error caught.
- `start({realm, cls, level})` starts a game. `realm` is 0 Logres, 1 Ériu or 2 Thule; `cls` is `warrior`, `archer`, `mage` or `priest`.
- `simulate(sec)` runs the fixed-step simulation and returns a war summary.
- `teleport(x, z)`, `setTod(p)` (time of day) and `look(yaw, pitch, dist)`.
- `frame()` renders one frame; `renderInfo()` returns draw calls, triangles and programs.
- `GAME`, `WAR`, `ENT`, `UI`, `CAM`, `DUN`, `INDOOR`, `BARROW`, `BARROW_STATE`, `INV`, `LOOT`.
- `dbg.*` exposes renderer internals; `fn.*` exposes most game functions.
- [walls] `walls` (`07g_walls.js`): `WALLS` (`stuck`, `fall`, `stats`), `shotHit(ax, ay, az, bx, by, bz, o)`, `wallsShotClear(e, t, arc)`, `wallsAimY`, `wallsProj`, `wallStrike`, `wallArrow`, `wallsStoneStep`, `wallMatOf`, `wallHouseOf`, `wallInRoof`. `breach` (`04g_breach.js`): `TBREACH` (`stats`: hits, breached, mended, fell), `list()` (the towers' sections), `sync`, `upload`.
- `heights` (attached by `03d_heights.js` at boot): `HTS` (the land before the shaping `H0`, `walk0`, what anyone could reach `reach`, the shaped texels `changed`, the keep mask), `stats()` (time, texels changed, blocked, filled, taken back, settled, hardy trees, crags), `was(x, z)` (the height before), `changedAt(x, z)`.
- `LORE` (attached by `10c_lore.js`): `openPrologue`, `openChronicle(section)`, `complete(stepId)`, `check()` (one look at the world for steps and notes), `progress()`.
- `guide` (attached by `10e_guide.js`, [guide]): `GUIDE` (`mark`, `near`, `stats`: notes held and shown), `GUIDE_WATCH`, `GUIDE_HELP`, `CHRON_EXTRA`, `mark()` (the next step's place now, how far and which way), `guideMarkOf(step)`, `guideWhere`, `guideAims`, `guideNoteIndex`, `guideMade(rec)`, `guideQuiet`, `chronGoals`, `chronGear`, `chronSieges`, `drawMinimap`.
- `guide2` (attached by `10f_guide2.js`, [guide2]): `G2` (`open`: the folds opened or shut; `noteFocus`; `point`: the button glowing now; `stats.pages[sec]`: a page's natural and folded height and the sheet's; `tap`: `until`, `downs`, `clicks`), `chronFold`, `chronNotes`, `g2MarkOf(step)`, `g2Pointer(step)`, `g2Scan(P)`, `guideWarAimHTML`, `g2TapHeld`, `g2LevelMark`, `g2MasterMark`, `g2WarTable`, `updateWarPanel`, `uiToast`, `halls()`, `nearHouse(P)`.
- `farm` (attached by `08h_farming.js`): `FARM`, `SEASON`, `seasonNow`, `setSeason(k)`, `farmTempAt`, `till(x, z, ang)`, `tillWhy(x, z)`, `sow(plotId, goodId)`, `step(dt)` (grow every bed by dt seconds), `harvest`, `saveSeed(plotId, slot)`, `newStrain(crop, traits)`, `plants`, `strips`, `herbs`, `learn`, `gather`, `dig`, `ctx()` (what the plant card shows and offers), `act(action)`, `save`, `load`.
- `CW` (attached by `08g_campaign.js`): campaign state and test hooks. `nature` (attached by `05h_nature.js`): tree, grass and crater hooks.
- `look3` (attached by `13v_look3.js`, [look3]): `LOOK3` (the halls' paths), `look3HallPlan()`, `MASON` (`list`, `stats`: spawned, climbed, worked, home, fled, stuck), `LOOK3S` (every scaffold's `ladders`, the sections and gaps that have one: `list`), `look3Masons(C, dt)`, `scaffolds()` (what each castle section's scaffold laid: standards, bays, boards, ladders, floors), `look3Clear(x, z, r, skip)`.
- `rubble` (attached by `04j_rubble.js`, [look4]): `RUBBLE` (each fallen house's mesh and what it laid: caps, ends, stones, timbers; `stats`), `HSCAF` (each house scaffold laid out: its standards' colliders, floors, ladder, `key`), `houseRubbleSync`, `houseRubbleGeo(H, level)`, `houseRubbleLevel(H)`, `houseRubbleRuns(H)`, `houseRubbleStub(H, run, i)`, `houseScafSync`, `houseScafLay`, `houseScafPts`, `houseScafLifts`, `houseScafWorkSpot(H, side)`, `stubAt(H, lx, lz)`, `wallTop`, `fell(H)`, `dims(H)`. `look4` (attached by `13w_look4.js`): `MAPL` (`last`: the map's names as placed, their boxes, which went on a pill, and the warbands' boxes, `bands`), `mapLabels`, `look4SiteAt`. The scaffolds' lifts are read on a section or gap: `W.l4n`, how many are up (`look4LiftsWanted(W)`, 04d_look4.js).
- `lookx` (attached by `13t_look.js`, [look]): `LOOK` (the towns' plans: green, tofts, paths), `lookTownPlan()`, `lookCoverAll()`, `cover(x, z)` (the cover map's value there, 0..1). (Not `look`: that is the camera's own `look(yaw, pitch, dist)`.)
- `bal3` (attached by `07c_balance3.js`): `BAL3` (counts: pursuits, dazzled misses, rushes held), `BODKIN_PIERCE`, `PURSUIT_CD`, `RUSH_SHARE`, `rushShare`, `DAZZLE`, `dazzleMissOf(level)`, `dazzled(e)`, `frostHeld`, `deepFrostHeld`, `blizzardHeld`, `classCanUse`, `npcWallWanted`, `pursuitOnHit`, `dazzleOnHit`, `dazzleMiss`, and for probes `canUse`, `effectiveSpeed`. `BAL.tap(src, dst, dmg, absorbed, opts)` (when set) hears every blow, heal (negative) and parry (`opts.parry`), and `BAL.track(e, dt)` every bot's step (`duels.py`).
- `bal5` (attached by `07c_balance5.js`, [balance5]): `BAL5` (counts: flares and flare tries, the press's healing, the skilled bots' steps spent getting clear), `flattenCraters()` (the measures' ground made whole: every crater undone, every grass fire out; returns the craters undone), `holyFlare(e, t)`, `flareWorth(t, e)`, `flareThreat(e)`, `castBreakable(t, left)`, `FLARE_REACH`, `hallowDazzle(G, o)`, `crippleWound(src, t, snare)`, `whirlHeal(e, n)`, `WHIRL_HEAL`, `ARCHER_REALM`, `PRIEST_REALM`, `botDodge(e, t, dt)`, `renewFresh`, `RENEW_FRESH`, and for probes `CLASSES`, `ABILITIES`, `healEnt`.
- `bal7` (attached by `07c_balance7.js`, [balance7]): `BAL7` (counts: Ériu ambushes among trees, Thule snares in the cold, the skilled bots' steps out of a volley), `B7T` (the rules' measures in one place; tests set them between runs to measure a rule against its absence), `plainArrowK(e)`, `ARCHER_GROUND`, `archerTrees(e)`, `archerColdAt(x, z)`, `archerWayHere(e, realm)`, `archerDrawK`, `archerHighK`, `archerWindK`, `archerAmbushK`, `archerHideT`, `archerHideWalk`, `snareCold`, `snareHold`.
- `bal6` (attached by `07c_balance6.js`, [balance6]): `BAL6` (counts: fires broken, rote renewals, held rushes, ward-kept spells), `breakFires(centres, r)`, `cleanArena(x, z, r, gone)`, `renewRoteCost`, `RENEW_ROTE_K`, `RENEW_ROTE_LEFT`, `WARD_GUARD`, `wardTakesBreak`, `spellGuarded`, `rushHeld`, `RUSH_HELD_K`, `MAGE_RANK`, `mageRankOf`, `archerPresser`, `archerCastsBy`, `botMageElbow`, `mageAoeWorth`, `STEADY_NEXT` (false), `steadyCast`, `castSteady`, `steadyNext`.
- `bal` (attached by `07c_balance.js`): `BAL`, `botMake`, `botStep`, `fairGear`, `applyFairGear(P, level)`, `ccAdjust`, `ccFactor`, `ccState`, `ccHudState`, `ccChampion`, `raiseOfferStep`, `raiseOfferAccept`, `raisePlayer`, `kingFresh()` (the Hollow King whole on his throne for a new fight), and for probes `canUse`, `segmentClear`, `PROJ`. `BAL.log = []` records every press the bots make. A bot given a `home` keeps to it when it kites or blinks (`botAwayYaw`).
- `cls` (attached by `07b_classes.js`): the callings' state (`state(e)`, `CLS.stats`), stances, blocks, the draw, elements, devotion and their test hooks.
- `gifts` (attached by `06c_gifts.js`): `GIFT`, `GIFT_DEF`, `giftHas`, `giftHarmMult`, `giftBuffAdjust`, `giftSpeedMult`, `useRealmGift`, `castGiftUlt`, `state()`. `land` (also attached there): `landSave`, `landApply`, `landKey`, `LAND.restored` (what the last load read back).
- `surv` (`09s_survival.js`): `SURV`, `FORAGE`, `CRAFT` (`CRAFT.stations`), `RECIPES`, `needsEat`, `needsDrink`, `eatGood`, `drinkAt`, `fishStart`, `survCraftOnce`, `openCraft` and more. `animals` (`08h_animals.js`): `A` (the `ANIMALS` state: `list`, `pens`, `down`), `anTame`, `anCommand`, `anBirth`, `penHas`, `animalsSave` and more. `build` (`06k_build.js`): `BUILD`, `BP`, `buildPlace`, `bValidate`, `bLandWhy`, `buildFenceLine` and more; the halls on `fn.halls()`.
- `seasons` (`05k_seasons.js`): `curves()`, `uniforms()`, `temps(x, z)` (the season map's against `farmTempAt`), `lie(x, z)`, `ice(x, z)`, `frozen()` (walkable ice texels per realm), `lakes()`, `fields()` (every field and garden, and any tree standing in one), `treesNear`, `step(sec)`, `seasonSettle`, `SEAS`, `SICE`. Set the year with `__hm.farm.setSeason(k)`, then `seasonSettle()` (or let a step notice the jump).
- `water` (`07b_swim.js`): `waterAt`, `state(id)` (depth, wading, swimming, soaked height, speed), `swimDims(id)`, `rivers()`, `ripple()`, `meshes()`, `tickWater(dt)` (the ripples without drawing the world), `splashAt`, `rippleDrop`, `swimAllowed`, `waterNear`, `RIVERS`, `PONDS`, `WATERGRID`; [water2] `water.dive` (`start`, `end`, `toggle`, `state()`, `finds()`, `take`, `cutHole`, `under()`), `water.boats` (`list()`, `jetties()`, `state(i)`, `board(i)`, `leave(spot)`, `moor(i, j)`, `phys(i, dt, thrust, turn)`, `hit(i)`, `ignite(i)`, `restore(i)`, `offers()`), `water.tracks` (`surface(x, z)`, `lay`, `prints(x, z, r)`, `shows(i)`, `drifts()`).
- Castles add `fn.castles()`, `fn.floorAt`, `fn.castleRoute`, `fn.castleSiegeHit` and `fn.CASTLE_PATCHED` (the building-shader anchors the castle patch found).
- [siege2] `siege2` (`04i_siege2.js`): `SIEGE2` (`stats`: towerMarks, curtainMarks, storms, through, stormFails, waits, sidesteps), `siegeMarkScore(W, x, z)`, `siegeMarks(C, x, z)`, `siegeTowerWayOk(W)`, `siegeStormRoute(W)`, `castleInside`. `palisade` (`04g_burn.js`): `PAL` (`list`: each burh's `cells` and `gaps`; `stats`), `PAL_T`, `palisadeInit`, `palisadeCellAt`, `palisadeFireAt`, `palisadeIgnite(cell, heat)`, `palisadeGaps(C)`, `updatePalisades`. `batter` (`04j_batter.js`): `BATTER`, `houseStoneHit(H, x, y, z, mult, src, fromAbove)`, `houseBlowAt`, `houseBreak`, `houseHoled`, `doorPose(rec)` (where an arrow stuck in a door is drawn now). `lines` (`07h_lines.js`): `LINE` (`on`, `stats`: held, by kind `heldDown`/`heldUp`/`heldFlat`/`heldUpUp`, switched, searches, found, arrived, waits), `wallsShotClearFrom`, `wallsHeld`, `wallsHeldShot`, `wallsFindLine`, `wallsSeekLine`.
- [siege3] `blaze` (`04g_blaze.js`): `BLAZE` (`list`: each burh's walk and the timber hallow's ring, `cells`; `gates`; `gaps`; `stats`: lit, spread, out, through, rebuilt, spared, harm, fell, gateLit, gateDown, crews, gaveUp, back, beats, playerBeats), `BZ_T`, `blazeInit`, `deckCellAt(x, y, z, r)`, `deckFireAt`, `deckFireCone`, `deckIgnite(c, heat, gen)`, `deckThrough(c, quiet)`, `deckMended(c)`, `gateFireAt`, `gateIgnite(G0)`, `gateFalls`, `updateBlaze`, `blazeUpload`, `blazeFiresOf(C)` (what burns at a castle: kind, where, where to stand to beat it, `beat(k)`), `blazeCrews`, `blazeFightStep`, `blazeOffers`, `blazeGapOn`, `blazeRouteCut`, `blazePlayerBeat`. `siege3` (`04i_siege3.js`): `SIEGE3` (`stats`: unstuck, trails, sidesteps, slotsMoved, holds, bursts), `bandSlotFree`, `bandSlot`, `bandUnstick`, `siegeStormHold`, `siegeOnBreach`. `rafters` (`04j_rafters.js`): `RAFTERS` (`list`: the holed houses' meshes), `houseRafterSync`, `houseRafterGeo`, `buildDoorRec`.

## Architecture

| Module | Role |
|---|---|
| `00_util.js` | Maths, noise and random helpers |
| `01_world.js` | World layout (realms, sites, lakes, navigation graph), heightfield, masks, colliders (`COLL`, `addBox`), `heightAt` / `isWalkable` / `zoneAt` |
| `01b_water.js` | Where the water stands: a river per realm carved down its valley into its lake (`RIVERS`), ponds in the closed hollows (a priority flood; other hollows are filled, so there are no dry pond beds; `PONDS`), `WATERGRID` (surface, flow and white water per 2 m texel), `waterAt(x, z)`, deep water in `W.walk` (2), `swimPassable`, `deepWaterAt`, `riverAt` |
| `02c_water_map.js` | The water map every shader can read (`GLSL_WATER_MAP`: `waterMapAt`, `rippleAt`, `causticNet`); the ground under and beside the water (`GLSL_WATER_GROUND`, called by the terrain shader: bed of sand, silt or gravel with a wobble, caustics, beach, the wet band the waves lap); the ripple simulation round the player (`stepRipples`, `rippleDrop`: high 256², medium 128², low off) |
| `03b_water.js` | The water surface: one mesh over every lake, pond and river at its own heights (no overlaps), see-through with absorption by depth, flow-mapped waves downstream, white water where rivers fall, shore foam, sky, clouds and far shore mirrored, rain rings and the ripple field |
| `02_render.js` | Renderer, `QUALITY_PRESETS`, shared uniforms `U`, `mat()` / `shareMat()`, GLSL chunks, sky, post-processing (bloom, light shafts) |
| `02b_nature_map.js` | The nature map: one texel per metre over the whole land for cut, burnt, burning and dug-up ground, uploaded to the GPU only where it changed |
| `02d_season.js` | The seasons' side of every shader: the uniforms `uSeasonA` (spring, summer, autumn, winter weights), `uSeasonB` (leaves turned, leaves fallen, blossom, fresh green), `uSeasonC` (flowers, summer gold, snow line shift, the air's temperature now), `uSeasonD` (apples, drifting leaves, fireflies), the season map `uSeasonMap` and `uSnowK`; `GLSL_SEASON`: `seasonMapAt`, `seasonTemp`, `snowHere`, `frostAt`, `seasonGrass`, `seasonLeaf`, `seasonTurnK`, `seasonFallen`, `twigCard` |
| `03_terrain.js` | Terrain, water, grass, flowers, trails, decals. [land] The terrain shader's rock lies in beds at the mountain's own scale (`landFacets`), with scree fans of angular stones set in grit (`talusAt`: two sizes, each stone smaller than its Voronoi cell and lying at its own tilt, so it never reads as paving), heather on the lower slopes, water streaks, damp gullies and the high snow by altitude, realm and season (one marked block after the rock block, and the `rk`, `nr` and `snow` lines) |
